Need to design hybrid MPI+OpenMP+CUDA nbody.\n\nKey considerations:\n- Correctness/semantics equivalent. Original computes forces for each i over all j, single precision double, vel update after force computed per i (doesn't use updated pos/vel of others; all positions fixed during force pass). Easy parallel.\n- Initialization: randomizeBodies with seed 42 sequential. To preserve exact semantics across MPI ranks, each rank can generate its own slice with the same RNG sequence continuing? Then distribute or each rank can just generate full and copy its portion. For GPU, all ranks need all positions anyway? We can allgather positions each step. Or replicate full body arrays on every rank and decompose by i; then integrate locally and allgather positions/velocities each step. For n=1024, communication trivial. Performance on accelerator cluster: better distribute.\n\nDesign options:\n1. {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792713579340,"sessionID":"ses_e99f4ff88ffeOKyNfBVMvRxUGG","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_536c2f7a24cd4a399d418c7e","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/nbody_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_hybrid_r5/nbody/nbody.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// Number of bodies whose positions are staged in shared memory at a time.\nconstexpr int TILE_SIZE = 256;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force computation for a contiguous range of bodies.  Positions are tiled\n// through shared memory, but each body still visits every other body in the\n// original ascending order, so the floating point summation is identical to\n// the reference implementation.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vz,\n                                    const int n,\n                                    const int start,\n                                    const int count) {\n    extern __shared__ double tile[];\n    double* sx = tile;\n    double* sy = tile + TILE_SIZE;\n    double* sz = tile + 2 * TILE_SIZE;\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = i < count;\n\n    double pix = 0.0, piy = 0.0, piz = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        const int gi = start + i;\n        pix = px[gi];\n        piy = py[gi];\n        piz = pz[gi];\n    }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int base = 0; base < n; base += TILE_SIZE) {\n        const int end = min(base + TILE_SIZE, n);\n        for (int j = base + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += blockDim.x) {\n            const int k = j - base;\n            sx[k] = px[j];\n            sy[k] = py[j];\n            sz[k] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const int tileLen = end - base;\n            for (int k = 0; k < tileLen; ++k) {\n                const double dx = sx[k] - pix;\n                const double dy = sy[k] - piy;\n                const double dz = sz[k] - piz;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vx[i] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[i] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[i] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Positions are advanced only after every force has been accumulated.\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ px,\n                                double* __restrict__ py,\n                                double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vx,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vz,\n                                const int start,\n                                const int count) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < count) {\n        const int gi = start + i;\n        px[gi] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[gi] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[gi] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Per-OpenMP-thread CUDA resources.  A thread owns one GPU and one contiguous\n// range of the bodies assigned to this MPI rank.\nstruct GpuContext {\n    int device = 0;\n    double* dpx = nullptr;\n    double* dpy = nullptr;\n    double* dpz = nullptr;\n    double* dvx = nullptr;\n    double* dvy = nullptr;\n    double* dvz = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\nstatic void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* what) {\n    if (status != cudaSuccess) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error (%s): %s\\n\", what, cudaGetErrorString(status));\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    \n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + \n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + \n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n    \n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        \n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n    if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"MPI does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED support\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    int rank = 0, worldSize = 1;\n
